## Call 1

```python
import numpy as np
print(sorted(W.keys()))
for k in sorted(W.keys()):
    a = W[k]
    print(k, a.shape, a.dtype, float(a.mean()), float(a.std()), float(np.abs(a).max()))
for l in range(4):
    for name in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o','W_fc','W_proj']:
        A = W[f'{name}.{l}']
        s = np.linalg.svd(A, compute_uv=False)
        print(l, name, 'top sv', np.round(s[:8],2), 'fro', round(float(np.linalg.norm(A)),2), 'stable rank', round(float((s**2).sum()/s[0]**2),2))
    for j in range(6):
        s_ = slice(64*j, 64*j+64)
        print('  head', j,
              'q', round(float(np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_q.{l}'][s_])),2),
              'k', round(float(np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_k.{l}'][s_])),2),
              'v', round(float(np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_v.{l}'][s_])),2),
              'o', round(float(np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,s_])),2))

```

## Call 2

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
for l in range(4):
    for n in ['ln1_w','ln1_b','ln2_w','ln2_b']:
        a=W[f'{n}.{l}']; o=np.argsort(-np.abs(a-np.median(a)))[:10]
        print(l,n,'median',round(float(np.median(a)),3),'outliers',list(zip(o.tolist(),np.round(a[o],2).tolist())))
print()
# per-head singular spectra
for l in range(4):
    for j in range(6):
        for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
            s=np.linalg.svd(W[f'{n}.{l}'][H(j)],compute_uv=False)
            print(f'L{l}H{j} {n}',s[:10],'... s20',round(float(s[19]),2),'s40',round(float(s[39]),2),'s64',round(float(s[63]),2))
        s=np.linalg.svd(W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,H(j)],compute_uv=False)
        print(f'L{l}H{j} W_o',s[:10],'... s20',round(float(s[19]),2),'s40',round(float(s[39]),2),'s64',round(float(s[63]),2))
print()
# top right singular vectors of W_k per layer: per-head energy and largest coordinates
for l in range(4):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W[f'W_k.{l}'],full_matrices=False)
    for i in range(3):
        e=[float((U[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()) for j in range(6)]
        c=np.argsort(-np.abs(Vt[i]))[:6]
        print(f'k L{l} sv{i} {S[i]:.2f} head-energy',np.round(e,2),'top coords',c.tolist(),np.round(Vt[i][c],2))
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W[f'W_q.{l}'],full_matrices=False)
    for i in range(3):
        e=[float((U[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()) for j in range(6)]
        c=np.argsort(-np.abs(Vt[i]))[:6]
        print(f'q L{l} sv{i} {S[i]:.2f} head-energy',np.round(e,2),'top coords',c.tolist(),np.round(Vt[i][c],2))

```

## Call 3

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99,184,383,153,63,266,165,163,52,5,45,86]
# W_o rows on special coords by head (energy)
for l in range(4):
    Wo=W[f'W_o.{l}']
    print('layer',l,'W_o row norm per head for special coords')
    for c in sp[:10]:
        print(c,[round(float(np.linalg.norm(Wo[c,H(j)])),2) for j in range(6)],'row total',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Wo[c])),2),'typical row',round(float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(Wo,axis=1))),2))
    Wp=W[f'W_proj.{l}']
    print('W_proj row norm', [ (c,round(float(np.linalg.norm(Wp[c])),2)) for c in sp[:10]],'median',round(float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(Wp,axis=1))),2))
    Wf=W[f'W_fc.{l}']
    print('W_fc col norm', [ (c,round(float(np.linalg.norm(Wf[:,c])),2)) for c in sp[:10]],'median',round(float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(Wf,axis=0))),2))
    for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        A=W[f'{n}.{l}']
        print(n,'col norm',[ (c,round(float(np.linalg.norm(A[:,c])),2)) for c in sp[:10]],'median',round(float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0))),2))
# largest-norm rows overall
for l in range(4):
    Wo=W[f'W_o.{l}']; Wp=W[f'W_proj.{l}']
    rn=np.linalg.norm(Wo,axis=1); o=np.argsort(-rn)[:12]; print('L',l,'W_o rows',list(zip(o.tolist(),np.round(rn[o],2).tolist())))
    rn=np.linalg.norm(Wp,axis=1); o=np.argsort(-rn)[:12]; print('L',l,'W_proj rows',list(zip(o.tolist(),np.round(rn[o],2).tolist())))
# spectra of whole matrices, effective with ln1_w
for l in range(4):
    for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        A=W[f'{n}.{l}']*W[f'ln1_w.{l}'][None,:]
        s=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)
        print(l,n,'eff sv idx 0,5,10,20,30,40,60,80,100,150,200,300,383:',s[[0,5,10,20,30,40,60,80,100,150,200,300,383]])
    A=W[f'W_fc.{l}']*W[f'ln2_w.{l}'][None,:]
    s=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)
    print(l,'fc eff',s[[0,1,2,3,5,10,20,30,40,60,80,100,150,200,300,383]])
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
mask=np.ones(384,bool); mask[sp]=False
def basis(A,r):  # top-r left singular vectors of A (columns)
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return U[:,:r]
print('eigen vs singular of K D^2 Q^T per head')
for l in range(4):
    D=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D
        K2=K.copy(); K2=K[:,mask]; Q2=Q[:,mask]
        M=K2@Q2.T
        ev=np.linalg.eigvals(M); sv=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
        o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))
        print(f'L{l}H{j} tr {np.trace(M):7.2f} sum|ev| {np.abs(ev).sum():7.2f} sum sv {sv.sum():7.2f} top sv {sv[:4]} top ev',np.round(ev[o[:6]],2),
              ' cos(Q,K)F',round(float((Q2*K2).sum()/np.linalg.norm(Q2)/np.linalg.norm(K2)),3))
# subspace overlaps Q vs K in each head, principal angles
print()
for l in range(4):
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)].T; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)].T
        BQ=basis(Q,25); BK=basis(K[mask],25)
        BQ2=basis(Q[mask],25)
        s=np.linalg.svd(BQ2.T@BK,compute_uv=False)
        print(f'L{l}H{j} Q/K top25 principal cos',s[:5],s[10],s[20],s[24])
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
# L0H3 eigen-phases
Q=W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0]; K=W['W_k.0'][H(3)]*D[0]
M=K@Q.T
ev=np.linalg.eigvals(M); o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o]
print('L0H3 |ev|',np.abs(ev[:50]))
print('phase deg',np.degrees(np.angle(ev[:50])))
for l,j in [(3,3),(3,0),(3,5),(1,0),(1,4),(2,1),(2,5),(0,0)]:
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]
    m=np.ones(384,bool); m[sp]=False
    M=K[:,m]@Q[:,m].T
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(M); o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o]
    print(f'L{l}H{j} |ev|',np.abs(ev[:24]))
    print(' phase',np.round(np.degrees(np.angle(ev[:24]))))
def sub(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return U[:,:r]
Vl0={j:sub(W['W_v.0'][H(j)].T,30) for j in range(6)}
Ol0={j:sub(W['W_o.0'][:,H(j)],30) for j in range(6)}
Ql0={j:sub(W['W_q.0'][H(j)].T,30) for j in range(6)}
print('baseline 30/384 =',30/384)
print('L0 subspaces mutual overlap (mean cos2 per dim) V3 vs Q3, V5 vs V3, O3 vs V3, O5 vs V3, O3 vs O5',
      [round(float(np.linalg.norm(a.T@b)**2/30),3) for a,b in [(Vl0[3],Ql0[3]),(Vl0[5],Vl0[3]),(Ol0[3],Vl0[3]),(Ol0[5],Vl0[3]),(Ol0[3],Ol0[5])]])
print('V0 vs V3,Q0 vs Q3',[round(float(np.linalg.norm(a.T@b)**2/30),3) for a,b in [(Vl0[0],Vl0[3]),(Ql0[0],Ql0[3])]])
# energy fraction of later Q/K/V (excl special coords) in each L0 head's subspaces
m=np.ones(384,bool); m[sp]=False
for l in [1,2,3]:
  for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
    for name,S in [('O3',Ol0[3]),('V3',Vl0[3]),('Q3',Ql0[3]),('O0',Ol0[0]),('V0',Vl0[0]),('O5',Ol0[5]),('V5',Vl0[5])]:
        fr=[]
        for j in range(6):
            A=(W[f'{n}.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l])[:,m]
            Sm=S[m]; 
            Qb,_=np.linalg.qr(Sm)
            fr.append(np.linalg.norm(A@Qb)**2/np.linalg.norm(A)**2)
        print(f'L{l} {n} in {name}',np.array(fr))
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99,184]
m=np.ones(384,bool); m[sp]=False
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
# spectra in details
for name,A in [('fc0 eff',W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]),('v0 eff',W['W_v.0']*D[0][None,:]),('q0 eff',W['W_q.0']*D[0][None,:]),('k0 eff',W['W_k.0']*D[0][None,:])]:
    s=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False); print(name,s[:80])
# per head projectors for V/Q/K/fc at L0
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
Ps=[]
for j in [0,3,5,1,2,4]:
    B,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(j)]*D[0],24); Ps.append(B@B.T)
Sum=sum(Ps); e=np.linalg.eigvalsh(Sum)[::-1]; print('sum proj V top24 per head eig',e[:50])
Ps2=[]
for j in [0,3]:
    for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        B,_=rows(W[f'{n}.0'][H(j)]*D[0],24); Ps2.append(B@B.T)
e=np.linalg.eigvalsh(sum(Ps2))[::-1]; print('sum proj q/k/v H0,H3',e[:50])
# which subspace do L0H3 Q and V occupy relative to each other? top 30
BQ,_=rows(W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0],35);BK,_=rows(W['W_k.0'][H(3)]*D[0],35);BV,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],35)
print('H3 Q/V principal',np.linalg.svd(BQ.T@BV,compute_uv=False)[:30])
print('H3 Q/K principal',np.linalg.svd(BQ.T@BK,compute_uv=False)[:35])
BV5,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(5)]*D[0],12)
print('H5 V vs H3 V',np.linalg.svd(BV5.T@BV,compute_uv=False))
print('H5 V vs H3 Q',np.linalg.svd(BV5.T@BQ,compute_uv=False))
# L0 heads 0,1,2,4,5 attention: Q top direction overlap
tops={}
for j in range(6):
    q,_=rows(W['W_q.0'][H(j)]*D[0],3); k,_=rows(W['W_k.0'][H(j)]*D[0],3); tops[j]=(q,k)
print('cos of top1 q across heads'); print(np.array([[float(tops[a][0][:,0]@tops[b][0][:,0]) for b in range(6)] for a in range(6)]))
print('cos of top1 k across heads'); print(np.array([[float(tops[a][1][:,0]@tops[b][1][:,0]) for b in range(6)] for a in range(6)]))
print('cos top1 q vs top1 k'); print(np.array([[float(tops[a][0][:,0]@tops[b][1][:,0]) for b in range(6)] for a in range(6)]))

```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
# eigenplanes of M for L0H3 in x-space:  score = x_i^T Dq... ; M_x = D Q^T K D, r=384 nonsym
Q=W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0]; K=W['W_k.0'][H(3)]*D[0]
Mx=Q.T@K
ev,vec=np.linalg.eig(K@Q.T)   # 64x64
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o]; vec=vec[:,o]
# right eigenvectors in x-space: Q^T vec
xv=Q.T@vec
V3,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],27)
V0,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(0)]*D[0],27)
V5,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(5)]*D[0],8)
fc,_=rows(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'][None,:],48)
for t in range(0,44,2):
    v=xv[:,t]; v=v/np.linalg.norm(v)
    print(t,'|ev|',round(abs(ev[t]),2),'phase',round(float(np.degrees(np.angle(ev[t]))),1),'in V3',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V3.T@v)**2),2),'in V0',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V0.T@v)**2),2),'in V5',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V5.T@v)**2),2),'in fc48',round(float(np.linalg.norm(fc.T@v)**2),2))
# who writes special channel
print()
for j in range(6):
    A=W['W_o.0'][sp][:,H(j)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(j)]*D[0])
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)
    print('H',j,'sp<-x gain sv',S,'left vecs',U[:,0],U[:,1])
    for r in range(2):
        v=Vt[r]; print('   inV3',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V3.T@v)**2),2),'inV5',round(float(np.linalg.norm(V5.T@v)**2),2),'in fc48',round(float(np.linalg.norm(fc.T@v)**2),2))
A=W['W_proj.0'][sp]
print('proj rows sv',np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False))
# top 3 of W_v.0 eff vs top of fc0
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']*D[0][None,:],full_matrices=False)
Uq,Sq,Vq=np.linalg.svd(W['W_q.0']*D[0][None,:],full_matrices=False)
Uk,Sk,Vk=np.linalg.svd(W['W_k.0']*D[0][None,:],full_matrices=False)
Uf,Sf,Vf=np.linalg.svd(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'][None,:],full_matrices=False)
print('v vs q top',(Vt[:4]@Vq[:4].T)); print('v vs k top',(Vt[:4]@Vk[:4].T)); print('v vs fc top',(Vt[:4]@Vf[:4].T)); print('q vs k',(Vq[:4]@Vk[:4].T))
print('head energy of v top3',[[round(float((U[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()),2) for j in range(6)] for i in range(4)])
print('head energy of q top3',[[round(float((Uq[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()),2) for j in range(6)] for i in range(4)])
print('head energy of k top3',[[round(float((Uk[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()),2) for j in range(6)] for i in range(4)])
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
m=np.ones(384,bool); m[sp]=False
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
V3,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],27)
V0,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(0)]*D[0],27)
fc,_=rows(W['W_fc.0']*W['ln2_w.0'][None,:],48)
print('V3 in fc48 principal',np.linalg.svd(V3.T@fc,compute_uv=False))
print('V0 in fc48 principal',np.linalg.svd(V0.T@fc,compute_uv=False))
print('V0 vs V3 principal',np.linalg.svd(V0.T@V3,compute_uv=False))
Qb,_=rows(W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0],30)
print('Q3 vs fc48',np.linalg.svd(Qb.T@fc,compute_uv=False)[:30])
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
print('phi sv',np.linalg.svd(phi,compute_uv=False)[:40])
phi0=W['W_o.0'][:,H(0)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(0)]*D[0])
print('phi0 sv',np.linalg.svd(phi0,compute_uv=False)[:40])
for T,name in [(V3,'T=V3'),(fc,'T=fc48')]:
  Phi=phi@T
  print(name,'| tr-cos tt pt tp pp (query reads first, key reads second), rel. norms')
  for l in [1,2,3]:
    for j in range(6):
        Q=(W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l])[:,m]; K=(W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l])[:,m]
        Tm=T[m]; Pm=Phi[m]
        qt=Q@Tm; qp=Q@Pm; kt=K@Tm; kp=K@Pm
        r=T.shape[1]
        def c(a,b):
            X=a.T@b; return float(np.trace(X)/(np.sqrt(r)*np.linalg.norm(X))), float(np.linalg.norm(X))
        o=[c(qt,kt),c(qp,kt),c(qt,kp),c(qp,kp)]
        print(f'L{l}H{j} tt {o[0][0]:6.3f} ({o[0][1]:5.1f}) q_prev*k_cur {o[1][0]:6.3f} ({o[1][1]:5.1f}) q_cur*k_prev {o[2][0]:6.3f} ({o[2][1]:5.1f}) pp {o[3][0]:6.3f} ({o[3][1]:5.1f})')
```

## Call 9

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
print('row norms W_fc.0 eff: pct',np.percentile(np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1),[5,25,50,75,95,99]))
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)
for r in [24,28,36,48]:
    Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=2000,tol=1e-4,random_state=0,fun='cube')
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y)
    kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0)
    print('r',r,'ICA kurtosis sorted',np.sort(kur)[::-1])
    print('   raw coordinates kurt',np.sort(kurtosis(Y,axis=0))[::-1][:12])
# heavy tail: how sparse are loadings
ica=FastICA(n_components=48,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-4,random_state=1,fun='logcosh')
Y=U[:,:48]*S[:48]
Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y); kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0); print('logcosh 48',np.sort(kur)[::-1])
ica=FastICA(n_components=28,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-4,random_state=1,fun='logcosh')
Y=U[:,:28]*S[:28]
Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y); kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0); print('logcosh 28',np.sort(kur)[::-1])
# Also W_fc.1 in full
A1=W['W_fc.1']*D2[1][None,:]
U1,S1,Vt1=np.linalg.svd(A1,full_matrices=False)
print('fc1 sv',S1[:70])
Y=U1[:,:60]*S1[:60]
ica=FastICA(n_components=60,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-4,random_state=1,fun='logcosh')
Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y); kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0); print('fc1 ICA 60',np.sort(kur)[::-1])
```

## Call 10

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
for l in range(4):
    A=W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:]
    n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); no=np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_proj.{l}'],axis=0)
    print('L',l,'fc-row norm hist',np.histogram(n,bins=[0,.3,.5,.8,1.2,1.6,2,2.5,3,4,6,20])[0],'proj-col norm hist',np.histogram(no,bins=[0,.1,.2,.3,.5,.8,1.2,2,3,5,20])[0])
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=n>1.0
print('alive',alive.sum())
Aa=A[alive]; 
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Aa,full_matrices=False)
print('alive sv',S[:60])
for r in [27,30,40,50]:
    Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y); kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0)
    print('r',r,'kurt',np.sort(kur)[::-1])
# cos-sim structure among alive neurons
Un=Aa/np.linalg.norm(Aa,axis=1,keepdims=True)
C=Un@Un.T; iu=np.triu_indices(len(C),1)
print('cos pct',np.percentile(C[iu],[0.1,1,5,50,95,99,99.9]))
print('fraction pairs |cos|>.5',float((np.abs(C[iu])>.5).mean()))
# k-means on unit vectors
for k in [10,20,28,40,60]:
    km=KMeans(n_clusters=k,n_init=5,random_state=0).fit(Un)
    print('k',k,'inertia',round(float(km.inertia_),1),'sizes',np.sort(np.bincount(km.labels_))[::-1][:15])

```

## Call 11

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=n>1.0
Aa=A[alive]
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Aa,full_matrices=False)
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O3=Uphi[:,:22]; V3r=Vphi[:22].T   # image (prev) and domain (cur) spaces
T=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],27)[0]
# top right singular vectors composition
for i in range(0,50,3):
    v=Vt[i]
    print(i,round(float(S[i]),2),'in T27',round(float(np.linalg.norm(T.T@v)**2),2),'in O3',round(float(np.linalg.norm(O3.T@v)**2),2))
print('fc alive top48 basis vs O3 principal',np.linalg.svd(Vt[:48]@O3,compute_uv=False))
print('fc alive top48 basis vs T27 principal',np.linalg.svd(Vt[:48]@T,compute_uv=False))
print('O3 vs T27 principal',np.linalg.svd(O3.T@T,compute_uv=False)[:22])
r=48
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
Sx=ica.fit_transform(Y)
# unmixing vectors in x-space
Wm=ica.components_           # r x r (acts on Y-rows)
u=(Wm@Vt[:r]) ; # rows in x-space (up to scaling) -- Y = Aa Vt^T so s = Wm (y) = Wm Vt A^T
u=u/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1,keepdims=True)
kur=kurtosis(Sx,axis=0); o=np.argsort(-kur)
fT=np.linalg.norm(u@T,axis=1)**2; fO=np.linalg.norm(u@O3,axis=1)**2
print('kurt',kur[o]); print('fT',fT[o]); print('fO',fO[o])
# phi image similarity
pu=(phi@u.T).T; pn=np.linalg.norm(pu,axis=1,keepdims=True); pu=pu/np.maximum(pn,1e-9)
C=np.abs(pu@u.T)
print('phi gain',pn[o,0])
best=np.argmax(C,axis=1); print('best match cos sorted',np.sort(C.max(axis=1))[::-1])

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=n>1.0; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
print('phi sv all top 60',Sphi[:60])
for r in [17,20,24,28]:
    O=Uphi[:,:r]
    Ap=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
    s=np.linalg.svd(Ap,compute_uv=False)
    print('r',r,'alive-rows with O removed sv',s[:40])
    s2=np.linalg.svd(Aa@O,compute_uv=False); print('   part in O sv',s2)
for j in range(6):
    print('H',j,'V sv',np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'][H(j)]*D[0],compute_uv=False)[:36])
    print('H',j,'W_o sv',np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.0'][:,H(j)],compute_uv=False)[:36])
# mapping from head-space: W_o^T W_o vs W_v W_v^T alignment for H3
Vh=W['W_v.0'][H(3)]; Oh=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]
print('tr(Vh Oh)/(|V||O|)',float(np.trace(Oh@Vh*D[0][None,:])/np.linalg.norm(Vh)/np.linalg.norm(Oh)))
```

## Call 13

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
# separate ICA in O-part and non-O part
AO=Aa@O                      # neurons x 19
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False)
r=29
Yr=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
icaT=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
ST=icaT.fit_transform(Yr)
icaO=FastICA(n_components=19,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
SO=icaO.fit_transform(AO)
print('kurt T',np.sort(kurtosis(ST,axis=0))[::-1]); print('kurt O',np.sort(kurtosis(SO,axis=0))[::-1])
# make skew positive
ST=ST*np.sign(((ST**3).mean(0))); SO=SO*np.sign(((SO**3).mean(0)))
print('skew T',((ST**3).mean(0))); print('skew O',((SO**3).mean(0)))
uT=(icaT.components_@Vt[:r]); uT=(uT.T/np.linalg.norm(uT,axis=1)).T
uO=(icaO.components_@O.T); uO=(uO.T/np.linalg.norm(uO,axis=1)).T
pT=(phi@uT.T).T; pT=(pT.T/np.linalg.norm(pT,axis=1)).T
C=np.abs(pT@uO.T)
print('phi(uT) vs uO cos: row max'); print(np.sort(C.max(0))[::-1])
print('assignment'); 
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment
ri,ci=linear_sum_assignment(-C)
print(list(zip(ri.tolist(),ci.tolist(),np.round(C[ri,ci],2).tolist())))
# neurons load: number of comps above threshold per neuron
thr=2.5
print('T comps per neuron hist',np.bincount((np.abs(ST)>thr).sum(1))); print('O comps per neuron hist',np.bincount((np.abs(SO)>thr).sum(1)))
print('neurons per T comp',(np.abs(ST)>thr).sum(0)); print('neurons per O comp',(np.abs(SO)>thr).sum(0))

```

## Call 14

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
AO=Aa@O; Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Yr=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
icaT=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); ST=icaT.fit_transform(Yr)
icaO=FastICA(n_components=19,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); SO=icaO.fit_transform(AO)
sT=np.sign((ST**3).mean(0)); sO=np.sign((SO**3).mean(0)); ST=ST*sT; SO=SO*sO
uT=(icaT.components_@Vt[:r]); uT=(uT.T/np.linalg.norm(uT,axis=1)).T*sT[:,None]
uO=(icaO.components_@O.T); uO=(uO.T/np.linalg.norm(uO,axis=1)).T*sO[:,None]
pT=(phi@uT.T).T; pT=(pT.T/np.linalg.norm(pT,axis=1)).T
Cs=pT@uO.T
ri,ci=linear_sum_assignment(-np.abs(Cs))
map_O2T={c:r_ for r_,c in zip(ri,ci)}
print('signs of phi match',[(int(r_),int(c),round(float(Cs[r_,c]),2)) for r_,c in zip(ri,ci)])
order=[int(x) for x in ri]  # T indices having O partner
rest=[i for i in range(29) if i not in order]
# G = sum over neurons of positive parts product  (prev comps as rows)
pos=lambda X: np.maximum(X,0)
G=np.zeros((19,29))
for m in range(19):
    for k in range(29):
        G[m,k]=(pos(SO[:,m]-1.0)*pos(ST[:,k]-1.0)).sum()
Gr=G[[int(np.where(ci==map_col)[0][0]) if False else 0 for map_col in []]] if False else None
rowsO=[int(ci[list(ri).index(t)]) for t in order]
Gs=G[rowsO][:,order+rest]
print('cur token index ->',order+rest)
print('rows: prev token (T idx):',order)
print((Gs/ (Gs.max()/9)).round(0).astype(int))
print('row sums',Gs.sum(1).round(0)); print('col sums',Gs.sum(0).round(0))
# Total neuron loading (energy) per token, as cur and as prev
print('cur token neuron energy',[ (t,round(float((ST[:,t]**2)[ST[:,t]>2].sum())),int((ST[:,t]>2).sum()),int((ST[:,t]<-2).sum())) for t in order+rest])
print('prev neuron energy',[ (int(map_O2T[m]),round(float((SO[:,m]**2)[SO[:,m]>2].sum())),int((SO[:,m]>2).sum()),int((SO[:,m]<-2).sum())) for m in rowsO])

```

## Call 15

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
# stability of T ICA across seeds and ranks; try r 26..32
def icaT(r,seed,rO=19):
    O=Uphi[:,:rO]
    Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False)
    Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=seed,fun='logcosh')
    s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
    u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T
    sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); return s*sg,(u.T*sg).T,kurtosis(s,axis=0)
base_s,base_u,base_k=icaT(29,0)
for r,seed in [(29,1),(29,2),(27,0),(28,0),(30,0),(32,0),(36,0)]:
    s,u,k=icaT(r,seed)
    C=np.abs(s.T@base_s)/len(base_s)
    print('r',r,'seed',seed,'best corr of each base comp',np.round(np.sort(C.max(0))[::-1],2))
# per-comp: neuron weight skew, mean positive/negative, neuron count for s>2 / s<-2; phi gain
pu=(phi@base_u.T).T; gain=np.linalg.norm(pu,axis=1)
print('phi gain',gain)
print('kurt',base_k)
print('pos count',(base_s>2).sum(0)); print('neg count',(base_s<-2).sum(0))
# Non-Gaussian structure: co-activation of T comps (shared neurons): classes of tokens
C=np.corrcoef(np.maximum(base_s-1,0).T)
print(np.round(C*10).astype(int))

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, pickle
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
b1={l:W[f'ln1_b.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
np.save('/tmp/u.npy',u)
idx=[i for i in range(29) if i!=9]
for l in [0,1,2,3]:
    print('=== layer',l)
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; V=W[f'W_v.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]
        qu=Q@u[idx].T; ku=K@u[idx].T; vu=V@u[idx].T  # 64 x 28
        qn=np.linalg.norm(qu,axis=0); kn=np.linalg.norm(ku,axis=0); vn=np.linalg.norm(vu,axis=0)
        S_=qu.T@ku/8
        # subtract row/col means
        Sc=S_-S_.mean(0,keepdims=True)-S_.mean(1,keepdims=True)+S_.mean()
        print(f'L{l}H{j} diag mean {np.mean(np.diag(Sc)):.2f} offdiag std {Sc[~np.eye(28,dtype=bool)].std():.2f}  max|S| {np.abs(S_).max():.2f}')
        print('  key-pref(col mean)',S_.mean(0)*10)
        if l==0: print('  |k(tok)|',kn); print('  |q(tok)|',qn); print('  |v(tok)|',vn)
print('idx',idx)
```

```python
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
```

## Call 18

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False)
QP,_=rows(W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0],30); KP,_=rows(W['W_k.0'][H(3)]*D[0],30)
V5,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(5)]*D[0],6)
V3,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],27)
def run(r,seed=0):
    Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=10000,tol=1e-6,random_state=seed,fun='logcosh')
    s=ica.fit_transform(Y); u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T
    sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); return s*sg,(u.T*sg).T
for r in [29,34,40]:
    s,u=run(r)
    k=kurtosis(s,axis=0)
    g=np.linalg.norm((phi@u.T).T,axis=1)
    inQ=np.linalg.norm(u@QP,axis=1)**2; inK=np.linalg.norm(u@KP,axis=1)**2; inV5=np.linalg.norm(u@V5,axis=1)**2; inV3=np.linalg.norm(u@V3,axis=1)**2
    o=np.argsort(-g)
    print('r',r)
    print(' gain',g[o]); print(' inV3',inV3[o]); print(' inQ3',inQ[o]); print(' inK3',inK[o]); print(' inV5',inV5[o]); print(' kurt',k[o].round(1))
    print(' pos',(s>2).sum(0)[o]); print(' neg',(s<-2).sum(0)[o])
    print(' n with gain>.3',(g>.3).sum())

```

## Call 19

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; other=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]<=0.3]
print('tokens',tok,'other',other)
# prev detectors: least squares unmix phi(u_tok) within alive-row O space: b = (AO) * pinv
Pt=phi@u[tok].T               # 384 x ntok (prev images)
# unmix: find vectors y_k in span(O24) s.t. y_k . Pt_j = delta_kj (dual basis) with O rank 24
O2=Uphi[:,:26]
Pc=O2.T@Pt
dual=np.linalg.pinv(Pc).T       # 26 x ntok dual vectors
# neuron prev-load on token k: a neuron's weight projected on Pt_k (i.e., response to prev=k)
resp_prev=(Aa@O2)@Pc            # neurons x ntok
resp_prev=resp_prev/resp_prev.std(0)
resp_cur=s[:,tok]
print('resp prev kurt',kurtosis(resp_prev,axis=0)); print('resp cur kurt',kurtosis(resp_cur,axis=0))
print('corr between resp_cur and resp_prev diag',np.array([np.corrcoef(resp_cur[:,k],resp_prev[:,k])[0,1] for k in range(len(tok))]))
pos=lambda X,t=1.0: np.maximum(X-t,0)
G=pos(resp_prev,1.5).T@pos(resp_cur,1.5)
# Normalize by row & col marginals
Gn=G/np.sqrt(np.outer(G.sum(1)+1,G.sum(0)+1))*len(tok)
print((Gn*2).round(0).astype(int))
# spectral: centered log G
L=np.log1p(G); L=L-L.mean(0,keepdims=True)-L.mean(1,keepdims=True)+L.mean(); np.fill_diagonal(L,0)
Us,Ss,Vs=np.linalg.svd(L)
print('sv',Ss[:8]); print('row sv1',Us[:,0]); print('col sv1',Vs[0]); print('row sv2',Us[:,1]); print('col sv2',Vs[1])
print('corr rowU1 vs colV1',np.corrcoef(Us[:,0],Vs[0])[0,1])

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, itertools
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; other=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]<=0.3]
Pt=phi@u[tok].T; O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@Pt
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
G=pos(rp,1.5).T@pos(rc,1.5)
Gd=G.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(Gd,0)
N=len(tok); name={i:t for i,t in enumerate(tok)}
tot=Gd.sum()
def frac(setV):
    v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[list(setV)]=True
    cc=Gd[~v][:,~v].sum(); vv=Gd[v][:,v].sum(); return cc/tot,vv/tot
res=[]
for k in [4,5,6,7]:
    best=[]
    for c in itertools.combinations(range(N),k):
        cc,vv=frac(c); best.append((cc+vv,c))
    best.sort(key=lambda t:t[0]); arr=np.array([b[0] for b in best])
    print('k',k,'random mean same-class frac',arr.mean().round(3),'std',arr.std().round(3),'min',arr[0].round(3),'z',round(float((arr[0]-arr.mean())/arr.std()),2))
    for b in best[:5]: print('   ',round(b[0],3),[name[i] for i in b[1]])
idx={t:i for i,t in enumerate(tok)}
Sv=[idx[c] for c in [1,17,20,21,23]]
print('S set frac (C-C, V-V)',frac(Sv),'rank among k=5 combos')
# V norm per head for all tokens, sorted by H0
for j,l in [(0,0),(3,0),(5,0),(1,0)]:
    Vv=W[f'W_v.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; print(j,'V norms',dict(zip(tok,np.round(np.linalg.norm(Vv@u[tok].T,axis=0)*10,1).tolist())))
# neuron pos-count / neg-count / total L1 of comps
print('pos',dict(zip(tok,(rc>2).sum(0).tolist()))); print('neg',dict(zip(tok,(rc<-2).sum(0).tolist())))
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def sub(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return U[:,:r]
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
other=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]<=0.3]; tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]
print('other',other)
OH=[sub(W['W_o.0'][:,H(j)],k) for j,k in [(0,14),(1,3),(2,3),(4,3),(5,8)]]
print('fractions in O-head subspaces H0(14),H1(3),H2(3),H4(3),H5(8)')
for i in other: print(i,[round(float(np.linalg.norm(u[i]@B)**2),2) for B in OH])
for i in tok[:6]: print('tok',i,[round(float(np.linalg.norm(u[i]@B)**2),2) for B in OH])
# H0 phi0 and images of tokens vs other comps
phi0=W['W_o.0'][:,H(0)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(0)]*D[0])
g0=np.linalg.norm(phi0@u.T,axis=0); print('phi0 gain',g0)
p0=phi0@u[tok].T; p0=p0/np.linalg.norm(p0,axis=0)
un=u[other]
print('cos phi0(tok) with other comps (rows tok, cols other)'); print(np.round(p0.T@un.T,2))
# Maybe H0 outputs ICA: alive rows in O0 subspace
B=sub(W['W_o.0'][:,H(0)],14)
Ao=Aa@B; print('alive rows in O0 sv',np.linalg.svd(Ao,compute_uv=False))
```

## Call 22

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def Mx(j):
    Q=W['W_q.0'][H(j)]*D[0]; K=W['W_k.0'][H(j)]*D[0]; return Q,K
Q3,K3=Mx(3)
# M acting in x-space: x_i^T Q^T K x_j. Eigendecomp of A=Q^T K (384x384 rank 64) via K Q^T
ev,vec=np.linalg.eig(K3@Q3.T)
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o]; vec=vec[:,o]
right=Q3.T@vec         # eigenvectors of Q^T K
planes=[]
t=0
while t<40:
    if abs(ev[t].imag)>1e-6:
        v=right[:,t]; a=np.real(v); b=np.imag(v)
        E,_=np.linalg.qr(np.stack([a,b],1)); planes.append((abs(ev[t]),np.degrees(np.angle(ev[t])),E)); t+=2
    else:
        t+=1
print('planes',len(planes))
print('H3 phase  |ev|      then for each other head rotation angle & mag in that plane')
rows=[]
for mag,ph,E in planes:
    line=[round(float(mag),2),round(float(abs(ph)),1)]
    for j in [3,0,1,2,4,5]:
        Q,K=Mx(j)
        B=E.T@(Q.T@(K@E))
        # decomposition into scaled rotation + rest
        c=(B[0,0]+B[1,1])/2; s_=(B[1,0]-B[0,1])/2
        ang=np.degrees(np.arctan2(s_,c)); m=np.hypot(c,s_)
        rest=np.linalg.norm(B-np.array([[c,-s_],[s_,c]]))
        line+= [round(float(m),2),round(float(ang),0),round(float(rest),2)]
    rows.append(line); print(line)

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; B={l:W[f'ln1_b.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def rows(A,r):
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False); return Vt[:r].T,S[:r]
V3,_=rows(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0],23)
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0]); Uphi,Sp,Vp=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False); O3=Uphi[:,:19]
for l in [1,2,3]:
    print('layer',l,'ln1_b sp',B[l][sp],'ln1_w sp',D[l][sp])
    # layer-level sp key directions
    U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W[f'W_k.{l}'][:,sp]*D[l][sp],full_matrices=False)
    print(' K_sp(eff) sv',S,'V',Vt[:2])
    for j in range(6):
        Ksp=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)][:,sp]          # reads h_sp directly (h already has w,b)
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]; Kfull=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]
        Uk,Sk,Vk=np.linalg.svd(Ksp,full_matrices=False)
        # query coupling
        out=[]
        for c in range(2):
            r=Uk[:,c]@Q                      # row in h-space : per unit h -> coupling
            rD=r*D[l]
            qb=float(Uk[:,c]@(Q@B[l]))        # constant query part from b
            rn=np.linalg.norm(rD)
            fT=np.linalg.norm(rD@V3)**2/rn**2; fO=np.linalg.norm(rD@O3)**2/rn**2
            out.append((round(float(Sk[c]),2),round(qb,2),round(float(rn),2),round(float(fT),2),round(float(fO),2)))
        # key non-sp norm
        nonsp=np.delete(Kfull,sp,axis=1)
        print(f' L{l}H{j} K_sp sv {Sk[:2]} (qb={out[0][1]},{out[1][1]})  |Q|along key dir={out[0][2]},{out[1][2]}  frac in T {out[0][3]},{out[1][3]} in O3 {out[0][4]},{out[1][4]}  |K nonsp|={np.linalg.norm(nonsp):.1f}')
```

## Call 24

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99]
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; B={l:W[f'ln1_b.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
# gate vector per head on h_sp
for l in [1,2,3]:
    print('layer',l)
    G=[]
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]
        qb=Q@B[l]
        g=K[:,sp].T@qb/8
        G.append(g); 
        # full key gate vector: qb^T K /8 over all coords
        gf=K.T@qb/8
        o=np.argsort(-np.abs(gf))[:6]
        print(f' H{j} |qb|={np.linalg.norm(qb):.2f} g_sp={g}  (full top coords {o.tolist()} {gf[o]}) norm-nonsp {np.linalg.norm(np.delete(gf,sp)):.2f}')
    G=np.array(G); print(' cos between heads g_sp'); print(np.round((G/np.linalg.norm(G,axis=1,keepdims=True))@(G/np.linalg.norm(G,axis=1,keepdims=True)).T,2))
# H3's first plane and H5 relation
Q3=W['W_q.0'][H(3)]*D[0]; K3=W['W_k.0'][H(3)]*D[0]
ev,vec=np.linalg.eig(K3@Q3.T); o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o]; vec=vec[:,o]
right=Q3.T@vec
a=np.real(right[:,0]); b_=np.imag(right[:,0]); E,_=np.linalg.qr(np.stack([a,b_],1))
M=Q3.T@K3
print('M in E',E.T@M@E)
A5=W['W_o.0'][sp][:,H(5)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(5)]*D[0])
print('A5 on E',A5@E)
AA=np.zeros((4,2))
for j in [1,2,4,5]:
    AA+=W['W_o.0'][sp][:,H(j)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(j)]*D[0])@E
print('A(1,2,4,5) on E',AA)
# energy of A5 inside E vs outside
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(A5,full_matrices=False); print('A5 sv',S); print('A5 right vec overlap with E',np.linalg.norm(Vt[:3]@E,axis=1))
# target: -b/w at L1
print('target -b/w L1',-B[1][sp]/D[1][sp]); print('target -b/w L2',-B[2][sp]/D[2][sp]); print('target -b/w L3',-B[3][sp]/D[3][sp])
for name,AAA in [('A5',A5@E),('Aall',AA)]:
    sol=np.linalg.lstsq(AAA,-B[1][sp]/D[1][sp],rcond=None); print(name,'fit coeff',sol[0],'resid',sol[1])
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sp=[21,39,58,99,184]
m_=np.ones(384,bool); m_[sp]=False
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
def Phi(m,h): return W[f'W_o.{m}'][:,H(h)]@(W[f'W_v.{m}'][H(h)]*D[m])
def cosF(A,B): return float((A*B).sum()/np.linalg.norm(A)/np.linalg.norm(B))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    for j in range(6):
        Q=(W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]); K=(W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l])
        Qm=Q*m_[None,:]; Km=K*m_[None,:]
        line=f'L{l}H{j} self cos(Q,K) {cosF(Qm,Km):6.3f} |'
        best=[]
        for m in range(l):
            for h in range(6):
                P=Phi(m,h)
                c1=cosF(Qm,Km@P); c2=cosF(Km,Qm@P)
                best.append((c1-c2,c1,c2,m,h))
        best.sort(key=lambda t:-abs(t[0]))
        print(line,' '.join(f'(L{b[3]}H{b[4]} q~kF {b[1]:.3f} k~qF {b[2]:.3f})' for b in best[:4]))

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
P=W['W_proj.3'].T   # neurons x 384
print('col norm pct',np.percentile(np.linalg.norm(P,axis=1),[1,25,50,75,99]))
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
# axis alignment of top right singular vectors
for i in list(range(0,30,3)):
    v=Vt[i]; o=np.argsort(-np.abs(v))[:4]; print(i,round(float(S[i]),1),'top coords',o.tolist(),np.round(v[o],2),'ipr',round(float((v**4).sum()),3))
base=None
for r in [24,26,28,30,32,36]:
    Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
    s=ica.fit_transform(Y); k=kurtosis(s,axis=0); sk=(s**3).mean(0)
    sg=np.sign(sk); s=s*sg
    print('r',r,'kurt',np.sort(k)[::-1].round(1)); print('    abs skew sorted',np.sort(np.abs(sk))[::-1].round(1))
    if r==30: base=s
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]
ut=u[tok]
Ncopy=W['W_o.3'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3])
o=Ncopy@ut.T     # 384 x 23
print('gain of copy on tokens',np.linalg.norm(o,axis=0)); 
on=o/np.linalg.norm(o,axis=0)
print('o cos matrix max offdiag',np.abs(on.T@on-np.eye(23)).max(), 'mean abs',np.abs(on.T@on-np.eye(23)).mean())
Up,Sp,Vp=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)
for r2 in [10,24,28,32]:
    print('r',r2,'fraction of o_c in W_proj.3 top-r',np.round((np.linalg.norm(on.T@Up[:,:r2],axis=1)**2),2))
print('baseline',[r2/384 for r2 in [10,24,28,32]])
# Does the copy head output subspace overlaps other L3 heads output
for j in range(6):
    Bj=W['W_o.3'][:,H(j)]
    Q_,_=np.linalg.qr(Bj); print('frac of o in span W_o H',j,np.round(np.linalg.norm(Q_.T@on,axis=0)**2,2).mean())
# W_proj.0..3 energy in the O-token span
Qo,_=np.linalg.qr(o)
for l in range(4):
    print('W_proj',l,'energy frac in span(o)',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Qo.T@W[f'W_proj.{l}'])**2/np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_proj.{l}'])**2),3),'W_o',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Qo.T@W[f'W_o.{l}'])**2/np.linalg.norm(W[f'W_o.{l}'])**2),3))
# Linear map M3
M3=W['W_proj.3']@(W['W_fc.3']*D2[3][None,:])
print('M3 sv',np.linalg.svd(M3,compute_uv=False)[:12])
B3=on.T@M3@ut.T
print('fraction token input read by W_fc.3',np.round(np.linalg.norm((W['W_fc.3']*D2[3][None,:])@ut.T,axis=0),1))
print('B3 (rows next c, cols cur a)'); print((B3).round(0).astype(int))
print('diag mean',np.mean(np.diag(B3)),'offdiag std',B3[~np.eye(23,dtype=bool)].std())
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg
mix=(ica.mixing_*sg[None,:])     # Y ~ s mixing^T + mean
vt=(mix.T@Vt[:r])                 # r x 384 : mixing vectors in x-space: row w_n = sum_k s_nk v_k
un=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; un=(un.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@(un.T/np.linalg.norm(un,axis=1)),axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]
b2=W['ln2_b.0']; bD=b2  # h = LN(x)*w + b; rows A already multiply by w; threshold from W_fc @ b
thr=W['W_fc.0'][alive]@b2
print('thresholds pct',np.percentile(thr,[1,10,50,90,99]))
# regress thresholds on sources: thr = sum_k s_nk * c_k + const
X=np.c_[s,np.ones(len(s))]; c,res,rk,sv=np.linalg.lstsq(X,thr,rcond=None)
print('R2',1-((thr-X@c)**2).sum()/((thr-thr.mean())**2).sum()); print('const',c[-1]); print('c tok',dict(zip(tok,np.round(c[tok],2).tolist())))
print('c other',dict(zip([i for i in range(29) if i not in tok],np.round(c[[i for i in range(29) if i not in tok]],2).tolist())))
# W_fc.0 raw (not eff) thr with removal of sp coords
sp=[21,39,58,99,184]; b3=b2.copy(); b3[sp]=0
thr2=W['W_fc.0'][alive]@b3
c2,_,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(X,thr2,rcond=None); print('no-sp R2',1-((thr2-X@c2)**2).sum()/((thr2-thr2.mean())**2).sum(),'const',c2[-1]); print(dict(zip(tok,np.round(c2[tok],2).tolist())))
# mean of s over neurons, and activity
print('thr by dominant token: mean thr of neurons whose top comp is k')
top=np.argmax(s,axis=1); print({int(k):(int((top==k).sum()),round(float(thr[top==k].mean()),2)) for k in tok})
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; B={l:W[f'ln1_b.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]
print('tok',tok)
P=phi@ut.T  # prev images
for l in [0,1,2,3]:
    print('L',l)
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]
        qb=Q@B[l]
        g_cur=(qb@(K*D[l])@ut.T)/8*10   # key preference for token at j (scale 10 ~ token norm)
        g_prev=(qb@(K*D[l])@P)/8*10     # key preference for prev-char image
        ks=(K*D[l])@ut.T; qs=(Q*D[l])@ut.T
        print(f' H{j} g_cur',g_cur,'| spread',round(float(g_cur.std()),2))
        print(f'    g_prev',g_prev,'| spread',round(float(g_prev.std()),2))
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; P=phi@ut.T; N=len(tok)
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@P
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
G=pos(rp,1.5).T@pos(rc,1.5)
mats={}
mats['G']=G.T   # cur x prev -> transposed: rows cur a, cols prev b
for l in range(4):
    F=(W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:])
    mats[f'C{l}']=ut@F.T@F@P       # rows cur, cols prev
    # L1-layer attention-based: Q(cur) K(prev)
Bq={}
for l in [1,2,3]:
    B=0
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]
        B=B+Q.T@K
    mats[f'qcur_kcur{l}']=ut@B@ut.T
    mats[f'qcur_kprev{l}']=ut@B@P
    mats[f'qprev_kcur{l}']=P.T@B@ut.T
mask=~np.eye(N,dtype=bool)
names=list(mats); X=np.array([ (mats[k]-mats[k].mean(0,keepdims=True)-mats[k].mean(1,keepdims=True))[mask] for k in names])
print(names)
print(np.round(np.corrcoef(X),2))
print('offdiag vs diag means'); 
for k in names: print(k,'diag',round(float(np.diag(mats[k]).mean()),3),'off',round(float(mats[k][mask].mean()),3),'off-std',round(float(mats[k][mask].std()),3))

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, itertools
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok)
P=phi@ut.T
Pn=P/np.linalg.norm(P,axis=0)
print('cos(phi u_c,u_c)',np.round(np.sum(Pn*ut.T,axis=0),2))
print('cos(phi u_c, u_b) offdiag max',np.abs(Pn.T@ut.T-np.diag(np.diag(Pn.T@ut.T))).max())
# Direct H3 head-level analysis: is W_o(H3) ~ pinv of V? does V3 map t->  (value) and then W_o maps to new subspace ?
# Purify: basis of T (all 29 comps) orthonormal
QT,_=np.linalg.qr(u.T)
Pperp=P-QT@(QT.T@P)
print('remaining fraction of P outside T span',np.round(np.linalg.norm(Pperp,axis=0)/np.linalg.norm(P,axis=0),2))
# ICA on neuron's weights in Pperp span
Ub,Sb,Vb=np.linalg.svd(Pperp,full_matrices=False)
print('Pperp sv',Sb)
rO=20
Bb=Ub[:,:rO]
AO=Aa@Bb
print('neuron part in Pperp sv',np.linalg.svd(AO,compute_uv=False))
ica2=FastICA(n_components=rO,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); so=ica2.fit_transform(AO)
sg2=np.sign((so**3).mean(0)); so=so*sg2
print('kurt O',kurtosis(so,axis=0).round(1))
uo=(ica2.components_@Bb.T); uo=(uo.T/np.linalg.norm(uo,axis=1)).T*sg2[:,None]
Pp=Pperp/np.linalg.norm(Pperp,axis=0)
C=Pp.T@uo.T
print('best token match per O comp',np.round(np.abs(C).max(0),2),np.argmax(np.abs(C),axis=0))
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment
ri,ci=linear_sum_assignment(-np.abs(C))
print([(tok[a],int(b),round(float(C[a,b]),2)) for a,b in zip(ri,ci)])
# correlate neuron's T comp and O comp: how diag now
Ccur=s[:,tok]
pp=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
GO=pp(so,1.5).T@pp(Ccur,1.5)
print('GO (rows O comps sorted by matched token, cols tokens)')
order=[int(ci[list(ri).index(i)]) if i in list(ri) else None for i in range(N)]
rowsO=[o for o in order if o is not None]
print(tok)
print((GO[rowsO]/GO.max()*9).round(0).astype(int))
print('matched tokens rows',[tok[i] for i in range(N) if order[i] is not None])
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, re, time
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=200)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@P
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
G=pos(rp,1.5).T@pos(rc,1.5)   # rows prev, cols cur
E=np.log1p(G); np.fill_diagonal(E,np.nan)
E=E-np.nanmean(E,axis=1,keepdims=True); E=E-np.nanmean(E,axis=0,keepdims=True); np.fill_diagonal(E,0)

py="""import os
import sys
from django db import models
class testuser testcase
    def setup self
        self client client
        self user user objects create user name test password secret
        self data
            name value
            type none
    def test get list self
        response self client get url
        self assertequal response status code
        self asserttrue len response data
    def get queryset self request
        qs super get queryset request
        if request user is superuser
            return qs
        return qs filter user request user
def parse args args none
    parser argparse argumentparser description
    parser add argument input type str default none help
    parser add argument output type str required true
    return parser parse args args
def main
    args parse args
    with open args input as f
        data json load f
    for key value in data items
        if isinstance value dict
            result key process value
        else
            result key str value
    print result
    return result
class model object
    def init self config
        self config config
        self layers
        for i in range self config num layers
            self layers append layer self config hidden size
    def forward self x mask none
        for layer in self layers
            x layer x mask
        return x
    def get name self
        return self name
    def set value self key value
        self values key value
        try
            self update key
        except keyerror as e
            raise valueerror e
if name main
    main
"""
def bigram(txt,M=24):
    voc=[c for c,_ in sorted({c:txt.count(c) for c in set(txt)}.items(),key=lambda t:-t[1])][:M]
    while len(voc)<M: voc.append('?')
    idx={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}
    C=np.zeros((M,M))
    for a,b in zip(txt,txt[1:]):
        if a in idx and b in idx: C[idx[a],idx[b]]+=1
    C=C+0.3
    p=C/C.sum(); L=np.log(p/np.outer(p.sum(1),p.sum(0))); np.fill_diagonal(L,0)
    return L,voc
def corr_score(E,L,pi,mask):
    X=L[np.ix_(pi,pi)][mask]; return np.corrcoef(E[mask],X)[0,1]
def sa(E,L,seed,steps=30000):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); M=L.shape[0]; perm=rng.permutation(M)
    mask=~np.eye(N,dtype=bool); Ez=E-E[mask].mean()
    def score(p): return (Ez*(L[np.ix_(p[:N],p[:N])]))[mask].sum()
    cur=score(perm); best=cur; bp=perm.copy()
    T0=abs(cur)/30+5
    for t in range(steps):
        T=T0*(1-t/steps)+1e-3
        i=rng.integers(N); j=rng.integers(M)
        if i==j: continue
        q=perm.copy(); q[i],q[j]=q[j],q[i]
        c=score(q)
        if c>cur or rng.random()<np.exp((c-cur)/T):
            perm=q; cur=c
            if c>best: best=c; bp=q.copy()
    return best,bp
mask=~np.eye(N,dtype=bool)
t0=time.time()
for name,txt in [('english',en),('romaji',ro),('python',py)]:
    L,voc=bigram(txt)
    res=[sa(E,L,s,12000) for s in range(4)]
    res.sort(key=lambda t:-t[0]); b,p=res[0]
    print(name,'chars',len(txt),'best score',round(float(b),1),'corr',round(float(corr_score(E,L,p[:N],mask)),3),'all',[round(float(r[0]),1) for r in res],'L std',round(float(L[~np.eye(24,dtype=bool)].std()),2))
    print('   map',{tok[i]:voc[p[i]] for i in range(N)},'time',round(time.time()-t0,1))
```

```python
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T

def show(name,X):
    X=X-X.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    Xn=X/np.linalg.norm(X,axis=0,keepdims=True)
    C=Xn.T@Xn
    iu=np.triu_indices(N,1); ord_=np.argsort(-C[iu])[:12]
    print(name,'top pairs',[(tok[iu[0][k]],tok[iu[1][k]],round(float(C[iu][k]),2)) for k in ord_])
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
Fc=W['W_fc.0']; A=Fc*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; dead=np.where(n<=1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok)
print('n dead',len(dead))
Fd=Fc[dead]
ym=Fd.mean(0); print('dead mean norm',np.linalg.norm(ym),'typical row',np.median(np.linalg.norm(Fd,axis=1)))
sv=np.linalg.svd(Fd-Fd.mean(0),compute_uv=False); print('dead rows sv',sv[:12])
print('dead mean top coords',np.argsort(-np.abs(ym))[:10],np.sort(np.abs(ym))[::-1][:10])
print('proj on tokens *100',dict(zip(tok,np.round(100*ut@ym,1).tolist())))
print('frac dead mean in token span',np.linalg.norm(np.linalg.qr(ut.T)[0].T@ym)**2/np.linalg.norm(ym)**2)
print('proj on all comps*100',np.round(100*u@ym,1))
# dead-row covariance in token span
Pd=(Fd-Fd.mean(0))@ut.T
print('dead rows std along token *100',dict(zip(tok,np.round(100*Pd.std(0),1).tolist())))
# Weight-decay-free: alive rows mean
ya=W['W_fc.0'][alive].mean(0); print('alive mean norm',np.linalg.norm(ya)); print('alive mean proj *100',dict(zip(tok,np.round(100*ut@ya,1).tolist())))
# same for L0H? output col: W_proj.0 columns of alive neurons vs dead
pn=np.linalg.norm(W['W_proj.0'],axis=0); print('proj col norm dead median',np.median(pn[dead]),'alive',np.median(pn[alive]))
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, itertools
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, leaves_list
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
Ncopy=W['W_o.3'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3]); o=Ncopy@ut.T
def C_of(X):
    X=X-X.mean(1,keepdims=True); Xn=X/np.linalg.norm(X,axis=0,keepdims=True); return Xn.T@Xn
Cs=[]
for l in [1,2,3]:
    F=np.vstack([W[f'W_q.{l}']*D[l],W[f'W_k.{l}']*D[l],W[f'W_v.{l}']*D[l]]); c=C_of(F@ut.T); Cs.append(c)
    c=C_of((W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:])@ut.T); Cs.append(c)
Cs.append(C_of(o))
Z=[c-np.diag(np.diag(c)) for c in Cs]
Z=[z/z[np.triu_indices(N,1)].std() for z in Z]
Cm=sum(Z)/len(Z)
L=linkage(np.array([np.delete(Cm[i],i) for i in range(N)]),'average',metric='correlation')
o_=leaves_list(L)
print('order',[tok[i] for i in o_])
print((Cm[np.ix_(o_,o_)]*10).round(0).astype(int))
for i in o_:
    idx=np.argsort(-Cm[i])[:4]; print(tok[i],[ (tok[j],round(float(Cm[i,j]),1)) for j in idx if j!=i])
# tightest cliques by size
iu=np.triu_indices(N,1)
for k in [3,4,5,6,7]:
    best=[]
    for c in itertools.combinations(range(N),k):
        sub=Cm[np.ix_(c,c)]; best.append((sub[np.triu_indices(k,1)].mean(),c))
    best.sort(key=lambda t:-t[0])
    print(k,[(round(float(b[0]),2),[tok[i] for i in b[1]]) for b in best[:4]])
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings

from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@P
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
G=pos(rp,1.5).T@pos(rc,1.5)
Gd=G.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(Gd,0)
ix={t:i for i,t in enumerate(tok)}
for name,Vs in [('V5',[1,3,19,23,24]),('S',[1,17,20,21,23]),('V+space17',[1,3,19,23,24,17])]:
    v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[[ix[c] for c in Vs]]=True
    tot=Gd.sum()
    print(name,'CV',round(float(Gd[~v][:,v].sum()/tot),3),'VC',round(float(Gd[v][:,~v].sum()/tot),3),'CC',round(float(Gd[~v][:,~v].sum()/tot),3),'VV',round(float(Gd[v][:,v].sum()/tot),3),'expected uniform CV',round(float(v.sum()*(N-v.sum())/(N*(N-1))),3))
print('per-row: top next tokens (prev -> cur), z over row')
Gz=(Gd-Gd.mean())/Gd.std()
for i in range(N):
    idx=np.argsort(-Gd[i])[:6]; print(tok[i],'->',[(tok[j],int(Gd[i,j])) for j in idx],'  rowH',round(float(-(Gd[i]/Gd[i].sum()*np.log(Gd[i]/Gd[i].sum()+1e-9)).sum()),2))
print('per-col: top prev tokens')
for j in range(N):
    idx=np.argsort(-Gd[:,j])[:6]; print(tok[j],'<-',[(tok[i],int(Gd[i,j])) for i in idx],'  colH',round(float(-(Gd[:,j]/Gd[:,j].sum()*np.log(Gd[:,j]/Gd[:,j].sum()+1e-9)).sum()),2))
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, itertools
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@P
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
def G_at(t): 
    g=pos(rp,t).T@pos(rc,t); np.fill_diagonal(g,0); return g
for t in [0.5,1.5,2.5]:
    Gd=G_at(t); tot=Gd.sum()
    res=[]
    for k in [5,6,7]:
        best_cv=(-1,None); best_cc=(9,None); vals=[]
        for c in itertools.combinations(range(N),k):
            v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[list(c)]=True
            cv=Gd[~v][:,v].sum()/tot; cc=Gd[~v][:,~v].sum()/tot; vals.append(cv)
            if cv>best_cv[0]: best_cv=(cv,c)
            if cc<best_cc[0]: best_cc=(cc,c)
        print('thr',t,'k',k,'max CV',round(float(best_cv[0]),3),[tok[i] for i in best_cv[1]],'uniform',round(k*(N-k)/(N*(N-1)),3),'min CC',round(float(best_cc[0]),3),[tok[i] for i in best_cc[1]],'unif CC',round((N-k)*(N-k-1)/(N*(N-1)),3),'mean/sd cv',round(float(np.mean(vals)),3),round(float(np.std(vals)),3))
```

```python
py="""import os
import sys
import json
from django db import models
from typing import list dict optional
class testuser testcase
    def setup self
        self client client
        self user user objects create user name test password secret
        self data
            name value
            type none
    def test get list self
        response self client get url
        self assertequal response status code
        self asserttrue len response data
    def get queryset self request
        qs super get queryset request
        if request user is superuser
            return qs
        return qs filter user request user
def parse args args none
    parser argparse argumentparser description
    parser add argument input type str default none help
    parser add argument output type str required true
    return parser parse args args
def main
    args parse args
    with open args input as f
        data json load f
    for key value in data items
        if isinstance value dict
            result key process value
        else
            result key str value
    print result
    return result
class model object
    def init self config
        self config config
        self layers
        for i in range self config num layers
            self layers append layer self config hidden size
    def forward self x mask none
        for layer in self layers
            x layer x mask
        return x
    def get name self
        return self name
    def set value self key value
        self values key value
        try
            self update key
        except keyerror as e
            raise valueerror e
    def to dict self
        return
            id self id
            name self name
            created at self created at isoformat
    def save self args kwargs
        if not self pk
            self slug slugify self title
        super save args kwargs
def test function
    assert foo bar true
    assert isinstance result list
    with pytest raises typeerror
        func none
    x np array
    y np zeros shape
    return np sum x y axis
@property
    def size self
        return len self items
if name main
    main
"""
py=py.replace('@','')
def bigram(txt,M=25):
    voc=[c for c,_ in sorted({c:txt.count(c) for c in set(txt)}.items(),key=lambda t:-t[1])][:M]
    idx={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}; M=len(voc)
    C=np.zeros((M,M))
    for a,b in zip(txt,txt[1:]):
        if a in idx and b in idx: C[idx[a],idx[b]]+=1
    p=(C+0.2)/(C+0.2).sum(); PMI=np.log(p/np.outer(p.sum(1),p.sum(0)))
    np.fill_diagonal(PMI,0)
    return PMI,voc
def sa(E,L,seed,steps):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); M=L.shape[0]
    Ez=E-E[mask].mean()
    perm=rng.permutation(M)
    def full(p):
        Lp=L[np.ix_(p[:N],p[:N])]; return float(((Ez*Lp)*mask).sum())
    cur=full(perm); best=cur; bp=perm.copy()
    T0=np.abs(Ez).sum()*L.std()/200; 
    for t in range(steps):
        Tm=T0*(1-t/steps)**2+1e-4
        i=rng.integers(N); j=rng.integers(M)
        if i==j: continue
        perm[i],perm[j]=perm[j],perm[i]
        c=full(perm)
        if c>=cur or rng.random()<np.exp((c-cur)/Tm): 
            cur=c
            if c>best: best=c; bp=perm.copy()
        else:
            perm[i],perm[j]=perm[j],perm[i]
    return best,bp
t0=time.time()
for name,txt in [('english',en),('romaji',ro),('python',py)]:
    L,voc=bigram(txt)
    res=[sa(E,L,s,25000) for s in range(10)]
    res.sort(key=lambda t:-t[0]); b,p=res[0]
    c=np.corrcoef(E[mask],L[np.ix_(p[:N],p[:N])][mask])[0,1]
    print(name,'chars',len(txt),'M',L.shape[0],'best',round(b,1),'corr',round(float(c),3),'top scores',[round(r[0],1) for r in res[:6]],'t',round(time.time()-t0))
    print('  map',{tok[i]:voc[p[i]] for i in range(N)})
```

```python
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
def best_cross(M,ks=(4,5,6,7,8)):
    M=M.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(M,0); tot=M.sum(); n_=M.shape[0]; out={}
    for k in ks:
        best=0;bc=None
        for c in itertools.combinations(range(n_),k):
            v=np.zeros(n_,bool); v[list(c)]=True
            cross=(M[v][:,~v].sum()+M[~v][:,v].sum())/tot
            if cross>best: best=cross;bc=c
        out[k]=(round(float(best),3),bc)
    return out
for t in [1.5,2.0,2.5,3.0]:
    G=pos(rp,t).T@pos(rc,t)
    r_=best_cross(G,(5,6,7))
    print('G thr',t,{k:(v[0],[tok[i] for i in v[1]]) for k,v in r_.items()})
# Text-based: small but use letter sets
en="the city was founded in the early nineteenth century by settlers from the united kingdom and became the capital of the province after the war of independence it is located on the northern bank of the river near the border with the neighbouring state and has a population of about three hundred thousand people according to the most recent national census the economy of the region is based mainly on agriculture mining and tourism although a number of manufacturing companies have moved to the area during the past twenty years he was born in london and studied mathematics at the university of cambridge where he received his doctorate in nineteen thirty two under the supervision of the physicist john taylor after graduating he worked as a research fellow and later joined the faculty of the institute of technology where he remained until his retirement his most important contributions were in the theory of electrical networks and he published more than one hundred papers and several books on the subject the film was directed by james walker and stars michael brown as a young police officer who investigates a series of murders in a small town it was released in the united states in march and received generally positive reviews from critics who praised the performances and the music but criticized the slow pace of the story"
ro="wagahai wa neko de aru namae wa mada nai doko de umareta ka tonto kentou ga tsukanu nani demo usugurai jimejime shita tokoro de nya nya naite ita koto dake wa kiokushite iru wagahai wa koko de hajimete ningen to iu mono wo mita shikamo ato de kiku to sore wa shosei to iu ningenchuu de ichiban douaku na shuzoku de atta sou da kono shosei to iu no wa tokidoki wareware wo tsukamaete nite kuu to iu hanashi de aru shikashi sono touji wa nan to iu kangae mo nakatta kara betsudan osoroshii to mo omowanakatta aru hi no kuregata no koto de aru hitori no genin ga rashoumon no shita de ameyami wo matte ita hiroi mon no shita ni wa kono otoko no hoka ni dare mo inai tada tokorodokoro niri no hageta ookina enmabashira ni kirigirisu ga ippiki tomatte iru rashoumon ga suzakuooji ni aru ijou wa kono otoko no hoka ni mo ameyami wo suru ichimegasa ya momieboshi ga mou nisannin wa arisou na mono de aru watakushi wa sono hito wo tsune ni sensei to yonde ita dakara koko demo tada sensei to kaku dake de honmyou wa uchiakenai kore wa seken wo habakaru enryo to iu yori mo sono hou ga watakushi ni totte shizen dakara de aru"
py="import os import sys from django db import models class testuser testcase def setup self self client client self user user objects create user name test password secret self data name value type none def test get list self response self client get url self assertequal response status code self asserttrue len response data def get queryset self request qs super get queryset request if request user is superuser return qs return qs filter user request user def parse args args none parser argparse argumentparser description parser add argument input type str default none help parser add argument output type str required true return parser parse args args def main args parse args with open args input as f data json load f for key value in data items if isinstance value dict result key process value else result key str value print result return result class model object def init self config self config config self layers for i in range self config num layers self layers append layer self config hidden size def forward self x mask none for layer in self layers x layer x mask return x def get name self return self name def set value self key value self values key value try self update key except keyerror as e raise valueerror e"
def table(txt,M=23):
    voc=[c for c,_ in sorted({c:txt.count(c) for c in set(txt)}.items(),key=lambda t:-t[1])][:M]
    idx={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}; M=len(voc)
    C=np.zeros((M,M))
    for a,b in zip(txt,txt[1:]):
        if a in idx and b in idx: C[idx[a],idx[b]]+=1
    p=(C+0.3)/(C+0.3).sum(); PMI=np.log(p/np.outer(p.sum(1),p.sum(0)))
    return np.maximum(PMI,0),C,voc
for name,txt in [('english',en),('romaji',ro),('python',py)]:
    Pm,C,voc=table(txt)
    rP=best_cross(Pm,(5,6)); rC=best_cross(C,(5,6))
    print(name,'PPMI',{k:(v[0],[voc[i] for i in v[1]]) for k,v in rP.items()},' COUNT',{k:(v[0],[voc[i] for i in v[1]]) for k,v in rC.items()})
```

```python
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)

ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)

gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
Ncopy=W['W_o.3'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3]); o=Ncopy@ut.T
print('tok',tok)
def pcs(name,X,k=4):
    X=X-X.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    X=X/np.linalg.norm(X,axis=0,keepdims=True)
    Ux,Sx,Vx=np.linalg.svd(X,full_matrices=False)
    fr=Sx**2/(Sx**2).sum()
    print(name,'var frac',fr[:6])
    for i in range(k):
        v=Vx[i]; o_=np.argsort(v)
        print(f'  PC{i}: neg',[(tok[j],round(float(v[j]),2)) for j in o_[:6]],' pos',[(tok[j],round(float(v[j]),2)) for j in o_[::-1][:6]])
pcs('copy_out',o)
pcs('copyV',(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3])@ut.T)
for l in [1,2,3]:
    pcs(f'fc{l}',(W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:])@ut.T,3)
    pcs(f'V{l}',(W[f'W_v.{l}']*D[l])@ut.T,3)
pcs('Kall1',(W['W_k.1']*D[1])@ut.T,3)
pcs('Qall1',(W['W_q.1']*D[1])@ut.T,3)
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, itertools
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
O2=Uphi[:,:26]; Pc=O2.T@P
rp=(Aa@O2)@Pc; rp=rp/rp.std(0); rc=s[:,tok]
ix={t:i for i,t in enumerate(tok)}
def sink(M,it=200):
    M=M+1e-3
    for _ in range(it):
        M=M/M.sum(1,keepdims=True); M=M/M.sum(0,keepdims=True)
    return M
pos=lambda X,t: np.maximum(X-t,0)
sets={'A':[1,3,19,23,24],'A+17':[1,3,19,23,24,17],'S':[1,17,20,21,23],'T5':[1,4,20,21,27],'T6':[1,4,20,21,23,27]}
for t in [1.0,1.5,2.0]:
    G=pos(rp,t).T@pos(rc,t); np.fill_diagonal(G,0); Gs=sink(G); np.fill_diagonal(Gs,0)
    # also rank-based: keep each neuron's top prev and top cur only
    tp=np.argmax(rp,axis=1); tc=np.argmax(rc,axis=1)
    keep=(rp.max(1)>t)&(rc.max(1)>t)
    H1=np.zeros((N,N)); 
    for a,b in zip(tp[keep],tc[keep]): H1[a,b]+=1
    H0=H1.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(H0,0); Hs=sink(H0+0.2); np.fill_diagonal(Hs,0)
    def stats(M,name):
        tot=M.sum(); vals=[]
        for c in itertools.combinations(range(N),5):
            v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[list(c)]=True; vals.append((M[v][:,~v].sum()+M[~v][:,v].sum())/tot)
        vals=np.array(vals); line=f'{name}: rand {vals.mean():.3f}+-{vals.std():.3f} max {vals.max():.3f} | '
        for k,c in sets.items():
            v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[[ix[x] for x in c]]=True
            cvx=M[~v][:,v].sum()/tot; vcx=M[v][:,~v].sum()/tot; vv=M[v][:,v].sum()/tot
            line+=f'{k}: CV {cvx:.3f} VC {vcx:.3f} VV {vv:.3f}; '
        print(line)
    stats(Gs,f'G sink thr{t}'); stats(Hs,f'Top-pair sink thr{t}')
    if t==1.5:
        print('neurons used',keep.sum()); print('H table (rows prev, cols cur) tokens',tok); print(H1.astype(int))
```
