```python
py="import os import sys from django db import models class testuser testcase def setup self self client client self user user objects create user name test password secret self data name value type none def test get list self response self client get url self assertequal response status code self asserttrue len response data def get queryset self request qs super get queryset request if request user is superuser return qs return qs filter user request user def parse args args none parser argparse argumentparser description parser add argument input type str default none help parser add argument output type str required true return parser parse args args def main args parse args with open args input as f data json load f for key value in data items if isinstance value dict result key process value else result key str value print result return result class model object def init self config self config config self layers for i in range self config num layers self layers append layer self config hidden size def forward self x mask none for layer in self layers x layer x mask return x def get name self return self name def set value self key value self values key value try self update key except keyerror as e raise valueerror e"
def sink(M,it=200):
    M=M+1e-3
    for _ in range(it):
        M=M/M.sum(1,keepdims=True); M=M/M.sum(0,keepdims=True)
    return M
def tab(txt,N=23):
    voc=[c for c,_ in sorted({c:txt.count(c) for c in set(txt)}.items(),key=lambda t:-t[1])][:N]
    idx={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}; C=np.zeros((N,N))
    for a,b in zip(txt,txt[1:]):
        if a in idx and b in idx: C[idx[a],idx[b]]+=1
    return C,voc
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
def maxcross(H):
    H=H.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(H,0); M=sink(H+0.2); np.fill_diagonal(M,0); N=len(M); tot=M.sum(); vals=[]
    for c in itertools.combinations(range(N),5):
        v=np.zeros(N,bool); v[list(c)]=True; vals.append((M[v][:,~v].sum()+M[~v][:,v].sum())/tot)
    vals=np.array(vals); return vals.mean(),vals.std(),vals.max()
for name,txt in [('en',en),('ro',ro),('py',py)]:
    C,voc=tab(txt); C0=C.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(C0,0)
    p=(C0+.3)/(C0+.3).sum(); PMI=np.log(p/np.outer(p.sum(1),p.sum(0))); np.fill_diagonal(PMI,-9)
    # scenario 1 : neurons pairs sampled prop to bigram count ^0.5
    for sc,w in [('count',C0),('sqrt',np.sqrt(C0)),('ppmi',np.maximum(PMI,0))]:
        pw=(w/w.sum()).ravel(); res=[]
        for rep in range(4):
            draws=rng.choice(pw.size,size=230,p=pw); H=np.bincount(draws,minlength=pw.size).reshape(23,23).astype(float)
            res.append(maxcross(H))
        print(name,sc,'mean-rand,std,max',np.round(np.mean(res,axis=0),3))
# null: independent
pw=np.ones(23*23)/(23*23); res=[]
for rep in range(4):
    draws=rng.choice(pw.size,size=230,p=pw); H=np.bincount(draws,minlength=pw.size).reshape(23,23).astype(float); res.append(maxcross(H))
print('null uniform',np.round(np.mean(res,axis=0),3))
```

```python
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
sets={}
for j in range(6):
    sets[f'o{j}']=W['W_o.3'][:,H(j)]

for r in [20,30,40]:
    Ps=0
    for k,M in sets.items():
        U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(M,full_matrices=False); Ps=Ps+U[:,:r]@U[:,:r].T

Up,Sp,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)
for j in range(6):
    Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.3'][:,H(j)],full_matrices=False)
    c=np.linalg.svd(Up[:,:32].T@Uo[:,:40],compute_uv=False)
    print('head',j,'principal cos W_proj.3 top32 vs W_o top40',c[:32])
# per W_proj singular vector: energy of W_o heads along it, normalised
Wo=W['W_o.3']
for i in range(36):
    v=Up[:,i]; e=[float(np.linalg.norm(v@Wo[:,H(j)])) for j in range(6)]
    print(i,round(float(Sp[i]),2),np.round(e,2))
```

## Call 46

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import spearmanr, kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok)
Ncopy=W['W_o.3'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3]); o=Ncopy@ut.T; on=o/np.linalg.norm(o,axis=0)
Up,Sp,Vp=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)
sc=np.linalg.norm(W['W_proj.3'].T@on,axis=0)          # MLP output strength along token output dir
# restrict: project on top-24 subspace
pr=Up[:,1:24]; pc=pr.T@on; eff=np.sqrt(((pc**2)*(Sp[1:24,None]**2)).sum(0))/np.linalg.norm(pc,axis=0)
nc=((s[:,tok]>2).sum(0)+(s[:,tok]<-2).sum(0))
ku=kurtosis(s[:,tok],axis=0)
g=gain[tok]; vout=np.linalg.norm(o,axis=0)
inv=np.linalg.norm(W['W_v.3'][H(4)]*D[3]@ut.T,axis=0)  # broad L3H4 reading
order=np.argsort(-nc)
print('tok ',[tok[i] for i in order]); print('n   ',nc[order]); print('kurt',ku[order].round(0)); print('s_c ',sc[order].round(1)); print('eff ',eff[order].round(1)); print('gain',g[order]); print('copy',vout[order]); print('L3H4',inv[order])
print('spearman n vs s_c',spearmanr(nc,sc)[0],' n vs eff',spearmanr(nc,eff)[0],' kurt vs eff',spearmanr(ku,eff)[0],' n vs gain',spearmanr(nc,g)[0],' n vs copy',spearmanr(nc,vout)[0],' n vs L3H4',spearmanr(nc,inv)[0])

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings

from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=240)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
Ncopy=W['W_o.3'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.3'][H(3)]*D[3]); o=Ncopy@ut.T
def C_of(X):
    X=X-X.mean(1,keepdims=True); Xn=X/np.linalg.norm(X,axis=0,keepdims=True); return Xn.T@Xn
sources={}
for l in [1,2,3]:
    for nm,M in [('q',W[f'W_q.{l}']*D[l]),('k',W[f'W_k.{l}']*D[l]),('v',W[f'W_v.{l}']*D[l]),('fc',W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:])]:
        sources[f'{nm}{l}']=C_of(M@ut.T)
sources['copy']=C_of(o); sources['prev']=C_of(P)
iu=np.triu_indices(N,1)
names=list(sources)
X=np.array([sources[k][iu] for k in names]); X=(X.T/X.std(1)).T
print(names); print(np.round(np.corrcoef(X),1))
Cm=sum(sources[k]/sources[k][iu].std() for k in names if k!='prev')/ (len(names)-1)
np.fill_diagonal(Cm,0)
e,v=np.linalg.eigh(Cm); o_=np.argsort(-e); e=e[o_]; v=v[:,o_]
print('eig',e[:8])
for i in range(4):
    idx=np.argsort(v[:,i]); print(f'EV{i}:',[(tok[j],round(float(v[j,i]),2)) for j in idx])
```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
H=lambda j: slice(64*j,64*j+64)
D={l:W[f'ln1_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}; D2={l:W[f'ln2_w.{l}'] for l in range(4)}
A=W['W_fc.0']*D2[0][None,:]
n=np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1); alive=np.where(n>1.0)[0]; Aa=A[alive]
phi=W['W_o.0'][:,H(3)]@(W['W_v.0'][H(3)]*D[0])
Uphi,Sphi,Vphi=np.linalg.svd(phi,full_matrices=False)
O=Uphi[:,:19]
Arest=Aa-Aa@O@O.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Arest,full_matrices=False); r=29
Y=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=8000,tol=1e-6,random_state=0,fun='logcosh'); s=ica.fit_transform(Y)
u=ica.components_@Vt[:r]; u=(u.T/np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)).T; sg=np.sign((s**3).mean(0)); s=s*sg; u=(u.T*sg).T
gain=np.linalg.norm(phi@u.T,axis=0)
tok=[i for i in range(29) if gain[i]>0.3]; ut=u[tok]; N=len(tok); P=phi@ut.T
V=[1,3,19,23,24]; v=np.array([t in V for t in tok])
Ph=P/np.linalg.norm(P,axis=0)
def blocks(M):
    M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True)-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)+M.mean()
    off=~np.eye(N,dtype=bool)
    return [M[np.ix_(v,v)][off[np.ix_(v,v)]].mean(), M[np.ix_(v,~v)].mean(), M[np.ix_(~v,v)].mean(), M[np.ix_(~v,~v)][off[np.ix_(~v,~v)]].mean(), M[off].std()]
print('blocks: [VV, V(cur/q) x C(prev/k), C x V, CC, std]')
for l in range(4):
    F=W[f'W_fc.{l}']*D2[l][None,:]
    if l==0: F=F[alive]
    print('fc',l,'cur-prev',np.round(blocks(ut@F.T@F@Ph),3),'cur-cur',np.round(blocks(ut@F.T@F@ut.T),3),'prev-prev',np.round(blocks(Ph.T@F.T@F@Ph),3))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    for j in range(6):
        Qm=W[f'W_q.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]; Km=W[f'W_k.{l}'][H(j)]*D[l]
        qt=Qm@ut.T; kt=Km@ut.T; qp=Qm@Ph; kp=Km@Ph
        print(f'L{l}H{j} qcur*kcur',np.round(blocks(qt.T@kt),2),' qcur*kprev',np.round(blocks(qt.T@kp),2),' qprev*kcur',np.round(blocks(qp.T@kt),2))
```
